```diff
+  if (browser.relatedTargetSessions == null) {
+    browser.relatedTargetSessions = new Set();
+  }
+  return browser.relatedTargetSessions;
+}
+
+/*
+Chrome auto-attach reaches only the direct children of the session it is
+called on, so each page, frame, and worker has to attach its own
+out-of-process iframes and workers
+(https://chromedevtools.github.io/devtools-protocol/tot/Target/#method-setAutoAttach).
+*/
+const RELATED_TARGET_FILTER = [
+  { type: "iframe", exclude: false },
+  { type: "worker", exclude: false },
+  { type: "shared_worker", exclude: false },
+  { exclude: true },
+];
+
+function attachRelatedTargets(browser, sessionId) {
+  const sessions = relatedTargetSessions(browser);
+  if (sessions.has(sessionId)) return Promise.resolve();
+  sessions.add(sessionId);
+  return browser
+    .send(
+      "Target.setAutoAttach",
+      {
+        autoAttach: true,
+        waitForDebuggerOnStart: true,
+        flatten: true,
+        filter: acceptLanguageEnUsEnabledForBrowser(browser)
+          ? [{ type: "service_worker", exclude: false }, ...RELATED_TARGET_FILTER]
+          : RELATED_TARGET_FILTER,
+      },
+      sessionId,
+    )
+    .catch((error) => {
+      sessions.delete(sessionId);
+      throw error;
+    });
+}
+
+function startsBeforeFirstDocument(target) {
+  return target?.type === "iframe" && target.waitingForDebugger === true;
+}
+
+export async function applyUaTreatmentToTarget(browser, sessionId, target) {
+  const hardwareProfile = hardwareProfileEnabledForBrowser(browser);
+  const acceptLanguageEnUs = acceptLanguageEnUsEnabledForBrowser(browser);

+      target.type === "service_worker" ? null : attachRelatedTargets(browser, sessionId),
+    ]);
+    return;
```

```diff
 export const DEFAULT_PRIMARY_PORT = 1337;
 export const DEFAULT_FORK_EXEC_BASE = 14000;
 // <<< box port table <<<
+export const WINDOW_USED_DIR = "/tmp/sand-window-used.d";
+export const WINDOW_USED_TOUCH_INTERVAL_MS = 15_000;
+export const WINDOW_IN_FLIGHT_SUFFIX = ".inflight";
+
+function touchFile(path) {
+  mkdirSync(dirname(path), { recursive: true });
+  closeSync(openSync(path, "a"));
+  const now = new Date();
+  utimesSync(path, now, now);
+}
+
+function removeFile(path) {
+  rmSync(path, { force: true });
+}
+
+export function clearStaleInFlightMarkers(dir = WINDOW_USED_DIR, remove = removeFile) {
+  let entries;
+  try {
+    entries = readdirSync(dir);
+  } catch (error) {
+    if (error?.code === "ENOENT") return [];
+    throw error;
+  }
+  const stale = entries.filter((entry) => entry.endsWith(WINDOW_IN_FLIGHT_SUFFIX));
+  for (const entry of stale) remove(join(dir, entry));
+  return stale;
+}
+
+export function createWindowUseMarker({
+  dir = WINDOW_USED_DIR,
+  intervalMs = WINDOW_USED_TOUCH_INTERVAL_MS,
+  now = Date.now,
+  touch = touchFile,
+  remove = removeFile,
+  onTouchFailed = () => {},
+} = {}) {
+  const lastTouchedAt = new Map();
+  const inFlight = new Map();
+  const attempt = (display, write) => {
+    try {
+      write();
+    } catch (error) {
+      onTouchFailed(display, error);
+    }
+  };
+  const mark = (display) => {
+    const at = now();
+    const last = lastTouchedAt.get(display);
+    if (last !== undefined && at - last < intervalMs) return;
+    lastTouchedAt.set(display, at);
+    attempt(display, () => touch(join(dir, String(display))));
+  };
+  return {
+    begin(display) {
+      const count = (inFlight.get(display) ?? 0) + 1;
+      inFlight.set(display, count);
+      if (count === 1) {
+        attempt(display, () => touch(join(dir, `${display}${WINDOW_IN_FLIGHT_SUFFIX}`)));
+      }
+      mark(display);
+    },
+    end(display) {
+      const remaining = (inFlight.get(display) ?? 1) - 1;
+      if (remaining > 0) {
+        inFlight.set(display, remaining);
+      } else {
+        inFlight.delete(display);
+        lastTouchedAt.delete(display);
+        attempt(display, () => remove(join(dir, `${display}${WINDOW_IN_FLIGHT_SUFFIX}`)));
+      }
+      mark(display);
+    },
+  };
+}
 
 function firstHeader(raw) {
   return Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;
```

```diff
+profile_browser_pids() {
+	local pid args
+	for pid in $(pgrep -x chrome 2>/dev/null); do
+		args="$( (tr '\0' '\n' <"/proc/${pid}/cmdline") 2>/dev/null || true)"
+		printf '%s\n' "${args}" | grep -q '^--type=' && continue
+		printf '%s\n' "${args}" | grep -qxF -- "--user-data-dir=${CHROME_PROFILE}" && printf '%s\n' "${pid}"
+	done
+}
+
+stop_profile_browser() {
+	local pids pid
+	pids="$(profile_browser_pids)"
+	[ -n "${pids}" ] || return 0
+	for pid in ${pids}; do kill -TERM "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true; done
+	for _ in $(seq 1 "${CHROME_STOP_WAIT_TENTHS}"); do
+		pids="$(profile_browser_pids)"
+		[ -n "${pids}" ] || return 0
+		sleep 0.1
+	done
+	echo "stop-window ${DISPLAY_NUM}: Chrome did not exit within the grace period; the unit sweep kills it" >&2
```

```diff
+# shellcheck shell=bash
+
+SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_PROC_ROOT="${SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_PROC_ROOT:-/proc}"
+SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_TERM_WAIT_TENTHS="${SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_TERM_WAIT_TENTHS:-30}"
+SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_PEER_USER="${SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_PEER_USER-box}"
+
+sand_desktop_unit_profile_pattern() {
+	printf -- '--user-data-dir=%s( |$)' "$(printf '%s' "$1" | sed 's/[][\.*^$+?(){}|/]/\\&/g')"
+}
+
+sand_desktop_unit_tagged_environs() {
+	local unit="$1" peer="${SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_PEER_USER}"
+	grep -slzxF -- "SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT=${unit}" "${SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_PROC_ROOT}"/[0-9]*/environ 2>/dev/null || true
+	[ -n "${peer}" ] && [ "$(id -u)" -eq 0 ] || return 0
+	id -u "${peer}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || return 0
+	setpriv --reuid="${peer}" --regid="${peer}" --clear-groups -- \
+		grep -slzxF -- "SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT=${unit}" "${SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_PROC_ROOT}"/[0-9]*/environ 2>/dev/null || true
+}
+
+sand_desktop_unit_profile_cmdlines() {
+	local profile="$1"
+	[ -n "${profile}" ] || return 0
+	grep -slzE -- "$(sand_desktop_unit_profile_pattern "${profile}")" \
+		"${SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_PROC_ROOT}"/[0-9]*/cmdline 2>/dev/null || true
+}
+
+sand_desktop_unit_pids() {
+	local unit="$1" profile="${2:-}" path pid
+	[ -n "${unit}" ] || return 0
+	{
+		sand_desktop_unit_tagged_environs "${unit}"
+		sand_desktop_unit_profile_cmdlines "${profile}"
+	} | while IFS= read -r path; do
+		pid="${path%/*}"
+		pid="${pid##*/}"
+		case "${pid}" in
+		'' | *[!0-9]*) continue ;;
+		esac
+		[ "${pid}" = "$$" ] && continue
+		[ "${pid}" = "${BASHPID:-}" ] && continue
+		printf '%s\n' "${pid}"
+	done | sort -un
+}
+
+sand_desktop_unit_signal() {
+	local signal="$1" pids="$2" pid
+	for pid in ${pids}; do
+		kill "-${signal}" "${pid}" 2>/dev/null || true
+	done
+}
+
+sand_desktop_unit_kill() {
+	local unit="$1" profile="${2:-}" pids
+	[ -n "${unit}" ] || return 0
+	pids="$(sand_desktop_unit_pids "${unit}" "${profile}")"
+	[ -n "${pids}" ] || return 0
+	sand_desktop_unit_signal TERM "${pids}"
+	for _ in $(seq 1 "${SAND_DESKTOP_UNIT_TERM_WAIT_TENTHS}"); do
+		sleep 0.1
+		pids="$(sand_desktop_unit_pids "${unit}" "${profile}")"
+		[ -n "${pids}" ] || return 0
+	done
+	for _ in $(seq 1 30); do
+		sand_desktop_unit_signal KILL "${pids}"
+		sleep 0.1
+		pids="$(sand_desktop_unit_pids "${unit}" "${profile}")"
+		[ -n "${pids}" ] || return 0
+	done
+	echo "sand-desktop-unit: ${unit} still has processes after SIGKILL: ${pids//$'\n'/ }" >&2
+	return 1
+}
```
